### Environment Interaction 14
----------------------------------------------------------------------------
```python
# We have the access_token. Now, let's get all recommended songs for the user.
# The API returns results in pages, with a default page_limit of 5 and a max of 20.
# We'll fetch all pages until no more songs are returned.

all_recommended_songs = []
page_index = 0
page_limit = 20

while True:
    recs = apis.spotify.show_recommendations(
        access_token=access_token,
        page_index=page_index,
        page_limit=page_limit
    )
    if not recs or len(recs) == 0:
        break
    all_recommended_songs.extend(recs)
    if len(recs) < page_limit:
        break
    page_index += 1

print(f"Total recommended songs: {len(all_recommended_songs)}")
print(all_recommended_songs[:3])  # Show a sample of the songs for verification
```

```python
# The keyword search for "play" did not reveal any playback or shuffle APIs either, likely due to truncation. Let's try to get the detailed API doc for a likely candidate: "show_playlist" (to see if it has shuffle or playback options), or try "update_playlist" in case it supports shuffling.
```

```
{
 "app_name": "spotify",
 "api_name": "update_playlist",
 "path": "/playlists/{playlist_id}",
 "method": "PATCH",
 "description": "Update a playlist title or privacy.",
 "parameters": [
  {
   "name": "playlist_id",
   "type": "integer",
   "required": true,
   "description": "The playlist id to update.",
   "default": null,
   "constraints": []
  },
  {
   "name": "access_token",
   "type": "string",
   "required": true,
   "description": "Access token obtained from spotify app login.",
   "default": null,
   "constraints": []
  },
  {
   "name": "title",
   "type": "string",
   "required": false,
   "description": "The updated title of the playlist.",
   "default": null,
   "constraints": [
    "length >= 1"
   ]
  },
  {
   "name": "is_public",
   "type": "boolean",
   "required": false,
   "description": "Whether the playlist is public or not.",
   "default": null,
   "constraints": []
  }
 ],
 "response_schemas": {
  "success": {
   "message": "string"
  },
  "failure": {
   "message": "string"
  }
 }
}
```
